Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}
is not of the form
{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\&{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}})}

and
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
exists.
Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
and consider the last case of the transition rule:

[GR-
\mu
]

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}}/{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}]\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
.

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})=\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}})=\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}}/{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}]})

so by the I.H,
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})\leq\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}}/{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}]})=\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
.

[GR-
\&
]

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}}\right\}}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}\&{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}})}
.
So,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}
or
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}
.
Now,
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})=\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})+1>\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})
.

[GR-
\oplus
]

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}\mathord{\oplus}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}})}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{j}}}}}\right\}}}
.
Now,
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})=\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})

if
({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}})\neq({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})
and

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})=1\leq\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
otherwise.

[GR-Ctx-I]

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}_{i}}
for
i\in I
.

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}})<\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
for
i\in I
so by the I.H.,

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}_{i}}})\leq\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}})<\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
for
i\in I
, so

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})\leq\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
.

[GR-Ctx-I’]

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in J}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}_{i}}
for
i\in J\subseteq I
.

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}})<\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
for
i\in J
so by the I.H.,

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}_{i}}})\leq\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}})<\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
for
i\in J
, so

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})\leq\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
.

[GR-Ctx-II]

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B^{\prime}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}}}\right\}}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B^{\prime}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime\prime}}}}}\right\}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime\prime}}
.
If
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}
,
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})=\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})=1
.
Otherwise,
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}})<\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
so by the I.H.,

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime\prime}}})\leq\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}})<\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
, so

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})\leq\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
.

∎

We need to proceed by induction on
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})

when trying to invert a projection onto
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}

with a receive from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
at the head
and a message from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
on the queue.
We know that the
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{depth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
exists for all
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}

by balancedness;
we would like a similar statement for
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}

and
\text{balanced}^{+}
.

\text{Balanced}^{+}
ensures the existence of

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
for all
({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mRoles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
.

Lemma 5

(En Route Transmissions are Bounded)
.

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
is
\text{balanced}^{+}
, then
for
({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mRoles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
,
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
exists.

Proof.

We show that if
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}>0
, then
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
exists by
induction on the structure of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{end}}
, then
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}=0
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}
, then
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}=0
.
If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}>0
, then

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}>0
so
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})
exists by I.H.,
so
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})=\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
, then

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}})}>0
for
i\in I
,
so
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}})
exists for
i\in I
by the I.H.,
so
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})=1+max_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}})
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}}\right\}}}

and
({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})\neq({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}})
, then

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}>0
,
so
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})
exists by the I.H.,
so
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})=1+\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}}\right\}}}
,
then
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})=1
.

Next we show that if
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
exists and

({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mRoles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
, then

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}>0
.
We proceed by induction on the structure of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{end}}
, then
({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})\not\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mRoles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}
, then
({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})\not\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mRoles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}
then
({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mRoles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}

and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}
exists, so by I.H.

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}>0
,
so
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}>0
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
, then

({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mRoles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}})}

and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}})}
exists for
i\in I
, so by I.H.

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}})}>0
for
i\in I
, so

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}>0
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}}\right\}}}

and
({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})\neq({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}})
, then

({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mRoles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}

and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}
exists, so by I.H.

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}>0
, so

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}>0
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}}\right\}}}
,
then
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}\geq 1
.

Now, if
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
is
\text{balanced}^{+}
then,
for
({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mRoles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
exists, so

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}>1
, so

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{mDepth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})
exists.
∎

4.2
Properties of Full Coinductive Merging

In order to use the full coinductive projection,
we first study basic properties of the full coinductive merge.
Following from previous work on projections,
we prove an operational
correspondence between a merged type and its components
(Lemma
7
)
and we prove that mergeability respects
set inclusions (Lemma
10
).
Additionally,
previous work on projections makes use
of the standard subtyping (
Def.

20
)
in their operational correspondence results.
The standard subtyping provides an
operational correspondence,
which the precise asynchronous subtyping lacks;
this makes the standard subtyping more useful
in determining typing context properties.
Once we define the standard subtyping,
we shall show that merging produces a subtype (Lemma
8
)
and preserves subtyping (Lemma
9
).

We will encounter merges
being done iteratively.
Under the view that merges produce witnesses
to compatibility,
surely we can
collapse these repeated applications
to a single one.
This property is
associativity.

Lemma 6

(Associativity of Merge)
.

Full coinductive merge is associative i.e. if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I_{j}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}
for all
j\in J
, then

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{j\in J,i\in I_{j}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{j\in J}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}}}
.

Proof.

Consider

\Re\ ={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{}}\cup\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccc}(\mathcal{T},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T})&\colon&\begin{array}[]{c}\bigcup_{i\in I}\mathcal{T}_{i}=\mathcal{T}\\
\forall i\in I,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\mathcal{T}_{i}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}}\right\rangle}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\colon i\in I\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right\rangle}\end{array}\end{array}\right\}\cup\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccc}(\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\colon i\in I\},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T})&\colon&\begin{array}[]{c}\bigcup_{i\in I}\mathcal{T}_{i}=\mathcal{T}\\
\forall i\in I,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\mathcal{T}_{i}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}}\right\rangle}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\mathcal{T}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right\rangle}\end{array}\end{array}\right\}

(54)

This satisfies the coinductive rules defining full merge, and so
\Re\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}}
.

i\in I
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}}\right)}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{k}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i,k}}\right\}_{k\in K}}

where for all
k\in K

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{j,k}}\colon j\in J_{i}\right\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i,k}}}\right\rangle}
.
Therefore,
\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{j,k}}\colon j\in J_{i},i\in I\right\}\Re\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}
.

[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{}}\text{-}\&
]

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{k}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}\right\}_{k\in K}}
, then

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{k}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{j,k}}\right\}_{k\in K_{j}}}
for all
j\in J_{i}
and
i\in I

where
K=\bigcup_{j\in J}K_{j}
and for all
k\in K

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{j,k}}\colon k\in K_{j}\right\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}}\right\rangle}
.
So for
i\in I
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{k}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i,k}}\right\}_{k\in K_{i}}}

where
K_{i}=\bigcup_{j\in J_{i}}K_{j}
and for all
k\in K_{j}

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{j,k}}\colon k\in K_{j},j\in J_{i}\right\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i,k}}}\right\rangle}
.
Therefore,
\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i,k}}\colon k\in K_{i}\right\}\Re\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}
.

∎

In the literature, the typing context transition relation is sometimes built up from
a local type transition relation, using additional rules for queue manipulation and
inter-participant communication.
We instead define context transitions directly (
Def.

9
),
which streamlines our proofs by avoiding this layered construction.
A separate transition relation on local types alone remains useful when
reasoning about syntactic properties of local types such as standard subtyping of local types alone without queues.
We therefore introduce local type LTSs
in order to state an operational correspondence for merge (
Lem.

7
),
which we use in turn to relate merging to standard
subtyping (
Lems.

8
and
9
).

Definition 19

(Local type LTSs)
.

We define the local type LTSs, denoted by

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
by the following rules:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}]\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\qquad{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}}})}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{j}}j\in I\qquad{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}}})}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{j}}j\in I\end{array}

Lemma 7

(Merge has an Operational Correspondence)
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}

for all
i\in I
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in I\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}
, then
there exists
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\colon i\in I\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
.

•

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in I\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
, then
there is some non-empty
J\subseteq I
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}
for
i\in J
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\colon i\in J\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
.

Proof.

Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in I\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right\rangle}
.

•

Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}
for all
i\in I
.
So, for
i\in I
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i,j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i,j}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,j}}\right\}_{j\in J_{i}}}
and there is
j\in J
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i,j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i,j}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}
.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in I\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right\rangle}
, so we may assume that
J_{i}=J
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i,j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i,j}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}}
for all
j\in J
.
Say
k\in J
has
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{k}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}

We also have that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{j}}\right\}_{j\in J}}
, where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,j}}\colon i\in I\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{j}}}\right\rangle}
for
j\in J
.
So,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\colon i\in I\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}}\right\rangle}
.
The proof in the case that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}
for all
i\in I
is similar.

•

Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
.
So,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}\right\}_{j\in J}}
and there is
k\in J
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{k}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in I\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right\rangle}
so for
i\in I
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,j}}\right\}_{j\in J}}
,
where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,j}}\colon i\in I\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}}\right\rangle}
for
j\in J
.
So,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,k}}
for
i\in I
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,k}}\colon i\in I\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}\right\rangle}
, and

\emptyset\not=I\subseteq I
.

•

Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
.
So,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}\right\}_{j\in J}}
and there is
k\in J
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{k}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in I\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right\rangle}
so there are
J_{j}\subseteq I
, non-empty, for
j\in J
such that
for
i\in I
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,j}}\right\}_{j\colon i\in J_{j}}}
,
where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,j}}\colon i\in J_{j}\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}}\right\rangle}
for
j\in J
.
So,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,k}}
for
i\in J_{k}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,k}}\colon i\in J_{k}\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}\right\rangle}
, and

\emptyset\not=J_{k}\subseteq I
.

∎

We shall now define the standard subtyping relation
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}

[
10
,
19
,
45
,
13
]
, on local types,
which is precise in the synchronous setting.
This standard subtyping is contained within the precise asynchronous subtyping.
We will use the standard subtyping during our proof of safety, deadlock-freedom, and liveness,
as there is an operational correspondence between
instances of the standard subtyping.
In fact, the operational correspondences from Lemma
7

will be sufficient to show that merge behaves well
with regard to the standard subtyping.

Definition 20

(Subtyping)
.

The
session subtyping relation
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
is coinductively defined:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B^{\prime}_{i}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I\cup{}J}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\lx@proof@logical@and\forall i\in I{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{<}{:}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B^{\prime}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{T}^{\prime}_{i}}}\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}]\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\qquad\par{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}}/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}]\\[8.61108pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}\qquad{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B^{\prime}_{i}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I\cup J}}\lx@proof@logical@and\forall i\in I{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{<}{:}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B^{\prime}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\end{array}

Note that the definition of
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
is far simpler than
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}
,
and this reflects in the fact that

\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
is computable
while
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}
is not
[
8
,
37
]
.
The intuition behind
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}

is that if you expect a type to
send a message from a set of messages,
then a type sending a message from
a smaller set will be permissible,
and similarly if you
expect a type to be able to
receive a message from a set of messages,
then a type receiving a message
from a larger set will be permissible.
In fact,
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
is sound and complete
with respect to local type transitions,
if we enforce that subtypes can always send fewer messages,
subtypes can always receive more messages,
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}
types (up-to unfolding)
are only related to other
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}
types.
It is now important to note that the
precise asynchronous subtyping
does not satisfy this operation correspondence.

Example 15

(Precise Asynchronous Subtyping does not provide an Operational Correspondence)
.

Recall the projection of the ring protocol onto
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}

and its optimisation.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{add}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\operatorname{int}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{add}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\operatorname{int}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{sub}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\operatorname{int}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}\right\}}\right\}}}}

(55)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}^{\text{\tiny opt}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{add}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\operatorname{int}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{add}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\operatorname{int}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}\right\}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{sub}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\operatorname{int}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{add}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\operatorname{int}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}\right\}}\right\}}}}

(56)

We have that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}^{\text{\tiny opt}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{add}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\operatorname{int}}}})}}}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}^{\text{\tiny opt}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{add}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\operatorname{int}}}})}}}
,
but

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\qquad\not\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{add}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\operatorname{int}}}})}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}^{\text{\tiny opt}}}\qquad\not\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{add}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\operatorname{int}}}})}}}
.
Thus, although
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}^{\text{\tiny opt}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}
, neither type can match the other’s immediate transition, so
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}
does not provide the operational correspondence enjoyed by standard subtyping.

Lemma 8

(Merge is a Subtype)
.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in I\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}
,
then for all
j\in I

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{j}}
.

Proof.

Consider
\Re\,=\{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}})\colon\exists\mathcal{T}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\text{, a set of local types, with }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\mathcal{T}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right\rangle}\}

This clearly satisfies the laws for subtyping by
Lem.

7
, so
\Re\subseteq\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
.
∎

Lemma 9

(Merge preserves Subtypes)
.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in I\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}

and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}
for all
i\in I
, then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}
.

Proof.

Consider
\Re\,=\,\{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}})\colon\exists\mathcal{T}\text{, a set of local types, such that }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\mathcal{T}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}\right\rangle}\text{ and }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}}\text{ for all }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}}\in\mathcal{T}\}
.
By
Lem.

7
, this clearly satisfies the laws for subtyping, so
\Re\subseteq\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
.
∎

A set of local types is mergeable if they are all ‘compatible’.
We should expect this ‘compatibility’ property to be decreasing
(with respect to subsets).

Lemma 10

(Mergeability respects Set Inclusion)
.

If
\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in I\}
is mergeable, then
\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in J\}
is mergeable for all non-empty
J\subseteq I
.

Proof.

Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in I\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}
.
We construct an LTS as follows:

1.

Start at node
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}:i\in I)
.

2.

At node
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}:i\in I^{\prime})
, if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}}
,
then let
I^{\prime\prime}=\{i\in I\colon\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i}}\}
.
Add an edge from
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}:i\in I^{\prime})
to
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i}}:i\in I^{\prime\prime})
labelled by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}
.

Now, let
J\subseteq I
be non-empty.
Remove all nodes,
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}:i\in I^{\prime})
, with
I^{\prime}\cap J=\emptyset
.
This graph is a correct type graph, so let
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{U}}
be the corresponding type.
We relabel the nodes.

1.

The root node is relabeled
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{U}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}:i\in J)
.

2.

If there is an edge from
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{U}^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}:i\in J^{\prime})
to
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i}}:i\in I^{\prime\prime})
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}
,
then there is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{U}^{\prime\prime}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{U}^{\prime}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{U}^{\prime\prime}}
.
Relabel
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i}}:i\in I^{\prime\prime})
to
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{U}^{\prime\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i}}:i\in I^{\prime\prime}\cap J)
.

The nodes of this graph satisfy the coinductive rules of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}}
,
so
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.2344,0.3516,0.5508}\operatorname{merge}}\left\langle{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\colon i\in J\}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{U}}}\right\rangle}
.
∎

5
Global and Local Type Asynchronous Association

Following the introduction of
LTS semantics for global types (
Def.

7
)
and typing contexts (
Def.

9
),
we establish a relationship between these two semantics
using the projection relation
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}}}
(
Def.

4
)
and the subtyping relation
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}
(
Def.

5
).
By analysing the structural consequences (
§​

5.1
)
of asynchronous subtyping
and projection,
we derive an operational correspondence
from this relation (
Thms.

11
and
12
).
Additionally, we use this relation
and the structural consequences of projection
to guarantee safety, deadlock-freedom, and liveness (
Thm.

13
).

Definition 21

(Association of Global Types and Typing Contexts)
.

A typing context
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is associated with a global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
written
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{a}}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}
,
iff

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
contains projections of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\right)}\supseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
; and

\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}\,\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}

such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}=({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\leqslant}}_{\text{a}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})}}
.

The association
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\cdot}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{a}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\cdot}}}
is a
binary relation over typing contexts
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
and global types
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
.
There are two requirements for the association:

(1)

the typing context
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
must include an entry for each role; and

(2)

for each role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
, its corresponding entry in the
typing context (
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}
) must be a subtype (
Def.

5
) of the projection of the global type
onto this role.

Looking again at the ring protocol example (
§​

1.2
),
we can observe how the transitions of the global type corresponds to updates in the local context.
This forms an
operational correspondence
between the global semantics and local process configurations.
Each global step is matched by a change in the local context.

\begin{array}[]{ccccccccccc}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{ring}}}&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\mathord{\oplus}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{add}}}}}&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{(1)}_{\text{ring}}}&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\mathord{\oplus}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{sub}}}}}&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{(2)}_{\text{ring}}}&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\&{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{add}}}}}&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{(3)}_{\text{ring}}}&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\&{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{sub}}}}}&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{(4)}_{\text{ring}}}&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathord{\oplus}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{sub}}}}}&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{(5)}_{\text{ring}}}\\
\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{a}}{}}$ }&&\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{a}}{}}$ }&&\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{a}}{}}$ }&&\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{a}}{}}$ }&&\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{a}}{}}$ }&&\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{a}}{}}$ }\\
