5.2
Completeness and Soundness

In
§​

5.1
,
we related local asynchronous semantics
to global semantics,
in a manner that can
be reflected by our projections,
via type contexts,
and in
§​

4.1
,
we determined properties
that being
\text{balanced}^{+}
ensures.
We can now combine these results;
we may now proceed by induction on
the depth functions provided by
\text{balanced}^{+}
,
to invert projections i.e. produce global contexts
from local contexts.

We begin by observing that
the semantics of a typing context
informs us of
the structure of its local types
and queues.

Lemma 18

(Inversion of Context Semantics)
.

Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\,\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
.

1.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\mathord{\oplus}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}})}

then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}=({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})
,
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}

where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}},\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}
for some
j\in I
.
And
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}=({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\left({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}},{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}\right)}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{j}})
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\right)}

for all
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\right)}
with
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
.

2.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\&{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}})}

then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}=({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})
,
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}

where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}},\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{j}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}
for some
j\in I
.
And
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}=({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\left({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}},{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B^{\prime}}}}})}\right)}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}})

with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B^{\prime}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{<}{:}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}
.
Finally,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}=({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{j}})
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}=({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}})

with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\right)}

for all
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\right)}
with
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\notin\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\}
.

Proof.

By rule-induction on

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}

(see
Def.

9
).
∎

As alluded to above,
we can proceed by induction on our depth functions
to match local sends and receives in projected types
to communications in global types.

Lemma 19

(Head Inversion of Projection)
.

Assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T})}}
.

1.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
, then

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)=\mathbb{G}[{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i,j}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}]_{j\in J}
where

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i,j}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{j}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i,j}})}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i,j}}\colon j\in J\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}
,

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i,j}}}\right)={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i,j}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbb{G}}\!\upharpoonright\!{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}=(\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{j}}}\}_{j\in J},[\cdot]_{J})
,
for
j\in J
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{j}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{j}}}}}}}
, and

\mathbb{G}
does not contain
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
.

2.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\left({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}},{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}\right)}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}})}}
, then

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)=\mathbb{G}[{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{j}}}}}\right\}}}]_{j\in J}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\right\}}

where

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{j}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{j}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{j}})}}
for all
j\in J
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{j}}\colon j\in J\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
,

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{j}}}\right)={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{j}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbb{G}}\!\upharpoonright\!{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}=(\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{j}}}\}_{j\in J},[\cdot]_{J})

for
j\in J
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{j}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{j}}}}}}}
, and

\mathbb{G}
does not contain
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
except as the sender of an en-route transmission.

3.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}})}}
with no element with destination
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
, then

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)=\mathbb{G}[{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i,j}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I_{j}}}]_{j\in J}
where

I_{j}\subseteq I
,

I=\bigcup_{j\in J}I_{j}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i,j}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{j}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i,j}})}}
for all
j\in J
and
i\in I_{j}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i,j}}\colon i\in I_{j}\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}
for all
i\in I
,

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i,j}}}\right)={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i,j}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbb{G}}\!\upharpoonright\!{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}=(\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{j}}}\}_{j\in J},[\cdot]_{J})

for
j\in J
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{j}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{j}}}}}}}
, and

\mathbb{G}
does not contain
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
.

Proof.

As
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
.
As
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
is balanced,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{depth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
exists.
We shall proceed by induction on
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{depth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
.
Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{depth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}=1
,
so
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
appears at the head of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
.

1.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
, then
by the definition of the projection,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}

where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}})}}
.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}[\cdot]_{l}}\!\upharpoonright\!{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}=(\{l\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\varnothing}}}\},[\cdot]_{\{l\}})
.

2.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}})}}

with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}
having no element with destination
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
,
then by definition of the projection,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}

where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}})}}
.

3.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\left({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}},{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}\right)}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}})}}
,
then by definition of the projection
and as
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{count}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
exists,
we have that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}}\right\}}}
,
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\right\}}
,
where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}})}}
.

Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{depth}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}>1
, so

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
is not at the head of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
.
We consider the cases for the head of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
.

1.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
where
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\not\in\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}\}
.
For
i\in I
, we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}})}}

and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}
, and
if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}})}}
then

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}}},\star)}}
.
We then apply the I.H.,
reindex the contexts, and
use associativity of the full coinductive merge to conclude.

2.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}}\right\}}}
where
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\not\in\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}\}
.
We have

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T})}}
, and
if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}})}}
then

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}}},\star)}}

where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}}}
, restricted to

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
, are the same.
We then apply the I.H. to conclude.

3.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}^{\prime}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}}\right\}}}
.
We have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T})}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\left({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}},{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}\right)}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}})}}
.
We then apply the I.H. to conclude.

∎

Since we are working with asynchronous subtyping,
the head of a local type is insufficient.
However by
Lem.

16
,
it will suffice to consider local asynchronous redexes
in their respective local contexts.
Further, it suffices to apply
Lem.

19

at each step to extend it to local contexts.

Lemma 20

(Inversion of Projection)
.

Assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T})}}

1.

If
\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)=\mathbb{L}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})}_{\oplus}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i,j}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i,j}}\right\}_{i\in I_{j}}}]_{j\in J}\langle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{j}}\rangle_{j\in K}

then

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)=\mathbb{G}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})}_{\oplus}[{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i,f(j)}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i,f(j)}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i,j}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I_{f(j)}}}]_{j\in J^{\prime}}\langle{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime\prime}_{j}}\rangle_{j\in K^{\prime}}

where
\sigma
is a reindexing of
\mathbb{L}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})}_{\oplus}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbb{G}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})}_{\oplus}}\!\upharpoonright\!{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}=(\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{j}}}\}_{j\in J^{\prime}\cup K^{\prime}},\sigma\mathbb{L}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})}_{\oplus})
, and

f(j)=k\in J\cup K\colon j\in\sigma k
for
j\in J\cup K
.
We additionally have that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i,j}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}_{j}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,j}})}}
for
j\in J^{\prime},i\in I_{f(j)}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{j}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}_{j}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}})}}
for
j\in K^{\prime}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i,k}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{j\in\sigma k}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i,j}}}}
for
k\in J,i\in I_{k}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{j\in\sigma k}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}}}
for
k\in K
,
where
\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i,k}}}\right)={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i,k}}
for
k\in J
and
i\in I_{k}
,

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}}\right)={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{k}}
for
k\in K
,

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i,j}}}\right)={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i,j}}
for
j\in J^{\prime}
and
i\in I_{f(j)}
,

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{j}}}\right)={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{j}}
for
j\in K^{\prime}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{j}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}_{j}}}}}}}
for
j\in J^{\prime}\cup K^{\prime}
.

2.

If
\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)=\mathbb{L}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})}_{\&}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i,j}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i,j}}\right\}_{i\in I_{j}}}]_{j\in J}

and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\left({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}},{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}\right)}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}})}}
,
then for all
j\in J
,

|I_{j}|=1
so relabel
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i,j}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{j}}
, also
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i,j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i,j}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}
for the unique
i\in I
,

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)=\mathbb{G}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}})}_{\&}[{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\overset{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}{\rightsquigarrow}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{j}}}}}\right\}}}]_{j\in J^{\prime}}

where
\sigma
is a reindexing of
\mathbb{L}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})}_{\&}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbb{G}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}})}_{\&}}\!\upharpoonright\!{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}=(\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{j}}}\}_{j\in J},\sigma\mathbb{L}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})}_{\&})
.
We additionally have that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{j}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}_{j}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}})}}
for
j\in J^{\prime}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{j\in\sigma k}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}}}
for
k\in J
,
where
\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}}\right)={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{k}}
,

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{j}}}\right)={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{j}}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}_{j}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}_{j}}}}}}}
for
j\in J^{\prime}
.

Proof.

Induction on local contexts.
The base case and inductive cases
are instances of
Lem.

19
.
∎

We will now be able to perform global transitions
upon observing local asynchronous transitions,
such that the subject of the transition
can still be projected onto a supertype of
the resultant local type.
To ensure that association is preserved,
we must also consider the other projections.
It can be easily observed that
performing transitions with subject
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}

does not drastically impact
the projection onto
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}(\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}})
.

Lemma 21

(Global Type Transitions)
.

Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T})}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
.

1.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\&{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}}

and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
, then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T})}}
.

2.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\&{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}}
, then

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\left({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}},{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}\right)}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}}}}}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma^{\prime}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T})}}
.

3.

Otherwise, there is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}})}}
.

Note that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\}}}\ni{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}
implies
that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
by Lemma
8
.

Proof.

Induction on the relation
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
,
making use of
Lem.

6
and
Lem.

10

to maintain the invariant specified in
Item

3
.
∎

Given a transition from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
associated to a
\text{balanced}^{+}
global type,
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
,
to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
,
we can now:
identify the redexes inside the projection of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
;
identify corresponding redexes inside
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
;
perform a corresponding transition of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
to
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
;
and project
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
to observe that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
is associated to
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
.
We state this formally, focusing on a single participant, in
Lem.

22
,
before applying this to contexts, proving
Thm.

11
.

Lemma 22

(Global Type Mirrors Local Actions)
.

Assume

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})}}\quad\text{with}\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\;\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}.

1.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
then
for any
k\in I
,
\exists{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\left({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}},{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B^{\prime}}}}})}\right)}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}})}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{k}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{<}{:}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B^{\prime}}}}

and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\mathord{\oplus}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B^{\prime}}}})}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
.

2.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{\left({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}},{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}})}\right)}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}})}}\quad\text{with}\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\;\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}
where

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{<}{:}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{k}}}}
for some
k\in I
, then

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}})}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}{\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathord{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\&{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B}}})}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}

3.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}
then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{\varnothing}}}

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}

Proof.

1.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
.
Applying
Lem.

16
, we have that

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)=\mathbb{L}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}})}_{\oplus}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i,j}}}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{T}^{\prime}_{i,j}}\right\}_{i\in I_{j}}}]_{j\in J}\langle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{T}^{\prime\prime}_{j}}\rangle_{j\in J^{\prime}}

with
I\subseteq I_{j}
for all
j\in J
and for all
i\in I
and
j\in J
,
we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{<}{:}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i,j}}}}
,
and if
\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}}\right)=\mathbb{L}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}})}_{\oplus}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{T}^{\prime}_{i,j}}]_{j\in J}\langle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbb{T}^{\prime\prime}_{j}}\rangle_{j\in J^{\prime}}

then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant_{\text{a}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}
.
Now we apply

to get that

\operatorname{\mathfrak{T}}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)=\mathbb{G}^{({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}})}_{\oplus}[{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i,f(j)}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.85}{{B_{i,f(j)}}}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i,j}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I_{f(j)}}}]_{j\in K}\langle{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime\prime}_{j}}\rangle_{j\in K^{\prime}}
.
We apply
